# Number ledger for paper/build/main.md and paper/build/supplement.md

Every number in the tables and every number written in digits in the text of the draft traces to a cited source: a result file, a run log, a code constant, a repository document or a public page, read 2026-11-07 to 2026-11-08. One line per number; text cells (setup descriptions, 'n/a' and 'not run' markers) are not numbers. Paths are relative to the repository root unless absolute. The dataset README was fetched from the Hub with the account token.
